### Moses and Monotheism — Sigmund Freud

Psychological-historical reconstruction, not devotional doctrine. V9: central inherited-memory/repression account rests on speculative reconstruction and psycho-Lamarckism challenged by Yerushalmi and later scholarship. Defenses by Derrida, Bernstein and Assmann recast transmission as archive/tradition/cultural memory; this preserves interpretive interest but changes rather than verifies the biological mechanism. D6 low from scholarly discussion and catalog: historical comparisons add ideas but no full-text density audit. Original whole book1939, earlier parts1937. ### The Honest Truth About Dishonesty — Dan Ariely

Evidence inspected: cited description, contents, review or author excerpt; density confidence low, no full-text audit. V9, high confidence: self-concept maintenance and moral reminders are recurring explanatory foundations in the book; 25 preregistered replications found no support for the prominent moral-reminder effect. Authors propose boundary conditions, which preserve a possible narrower effect but not the confidently generalized intervention. No personal misconduct conclusion is needed. D7: distinct contexts add learning; unresolved central evidence limits its use. Assessment confidence: low. Eligibility confidence: moderate; explicit gate confidence stated separately.
